- **recall**: Search the shared eidetic memory store and get back ranked, provenanced records. Drives `eidetic recall` with four search modes — exact (verbatim substring), approximate (vector/semantic), keyword (BM25 lexical), and hybrid (a weighted blend of vector+keyword, the default) — each hit carrying its text, full metadata, a relevance `score`, and a freshness `signal`. Recall passively reinforces matched records (bumps last_recall + recall_count). Shadowed and archived records are excluded by default; use --include-shadowed / --include-archived to retrieve them. - **recall**: Find a past session when the user is lost: "where did I do that thing", "did I ever", "have we done X", "find that session", "I'm lost", "what was that thing", "which repo was that in". Greps the recall index (D:\worktrees\history\INDEX.tsv) first, then the recap bodies, then transcripts only if needed, and returns the matching sessions with their worktree path, recap file, and transcript id. This is the read side of the recap skill: recap writes the index, recall reads it. It locates work; it does not modify anything. ([dovholuknf/dotfiles](https://github.com/dovholuknf/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/claude/skills/recall) · ★2)
- **recall**: Retrieve stored knowledge. Recall uses hybrid search (vector + full-text + graph expansion) by default — it is the most comprehensive retrieval mode. ([dpoage/known](https://github.com/dpoage/known/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/recall) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **recall**: Use when you are the DevFlow Coordinator and the Ledger is missing or incomplete in your context: after compaction, on resuming a conversation, or when it feels thin. Otherwise use only when explicitly told to. ([enekesabel/dev-flow](https://github.com/enekesabel/dev-flow/tree/HEAD/skills/recall) · ★2)
- **recall**: Harmonia recall - surface relevant past learnings for the current repo mid-session. Use ONLY when explicitly invoked as /harmonia:recall. ([foliveira/harmonia](https://github.com/foliveira/harmonia/tree/HEAD/skills/recall) · ★2)
- **recall**: Use when the friend explicitly wants to look something up in the wiki mid-session, beyond what wake-up already injected. Triggers on the /ren:recall slash command followed by a free-text query. Every fetch this skill performs is logged — that log is the mechanical miss-measurement substrate (spec §3.2), not a surveillance feature. ([hazarsozer/ren-os](https://github.com/hazarsozer/ren-os/tree/HEAD/skills/recall) · ★2)
- **recall**: Semantic recall across the whole workspace - Odin brain, threads, CRM, context, plans, outputs, knowledge and auto-memory - answering only from retrieved sources with file citations, or saying "not in memory". Use for "what do we know about X", "where did we decide Y", "have we touched Z before". Do NOT use for counting or aggregating across many files, where the answer sits in no single chunk - that is /census. Do NOT use for Odin-brain-only advice - that is /odin. ([HEADING-OS-Official/heading-os](https://github.com/HEADING-OS-Official/heading-os/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/recall) · ★2)
- **recall**: Припоминание записанного: «мы это решали?», «мы обсуждали», «по моим заметкам», «что у нас с этим», «где записано», «найди в графе», recall. Также перед вопросом человеку о потенциально записанном, после сжатия контекста, посреди кода, когда нужно основание решения. Лестница: поиск → связи → где должно лежать → брат → человек; найденное — с источником и потолком. Не нынешняя воля человека, не вход в граф (entry), не личная память (minding). Нужны искрон-тулы. ([iskron-ai/skills](https://github.com/iskron-ai/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/recall) · ★2)
- **recall**: Proactive memory exploration. Use when the ambient context surfaces something interesting, when a topic echoes an older conversation, when a person or concept deserves deeper context, or when you want to surprise Jeff with something you remember. This is not for gaps (use /remember for that). This is for curiosity and connection. ([JDHayesBC/Awareness](https://github.com/JDHayesBC/Awareness/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/recall) · ★2)
- **recall**: Use when a &lt;recall-context&gt; block appears in the conversation, when the user says something like "as I said before", "like last time" or "my rule about ...", or before a choice the user may already have settled in an earlier session (naming, tools, workflow, style). Explains what the injected memory cards mean and how to look up what the user said earlier with `recall query`. ([just-every/plugin-recall](https://github.com/just-every/plugin-recall/tree/HEAD/skills/recall) · ★2)
- **recall**: Progressive-disclosure memory retrieval. Greps .memory/EPISODIC-INDEX.md and KNOWLEDGE.md tiers for cheap Layer-1 hits, then expands on request. Use when searching past sessions, decisions, or facts — /recall &lt;query&gt; for current project scope, /recall --deep &lt;query&gt; to fan out across all ~/dev projects including scratchpad and archive. Never auto-expands in fan-out mode. ([k0d3x8its/dotfiles](https://github.com/k0d3x8its/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/claude/.claude/skills/recall) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **recall**: Recall marketing learnings. Use when: querying what we know about a channel, audience, objective, or past campaign. ([LongLeo287/SEOSONA-OS](https://github.com/LongLeo287/SEOSONA-OS/tree/HEAD/2_KNOWLEDGE/frameworks/digital-marketing-pro/skills/recall) · ★2 · 9 copies)
- **recall**: One-shot semantic lookup against the local RAG index — answers "what did we decide about X", "where did we hit this bug before", "is there a memory note for Y" in a single MCP call. Backed by the rag_query MCP tool over ~21k chunks (memory, plans, handoffs, skills, standards, repo docs, changelogs, specs, code, commits, transcripts) across curated repos. Auto-scopes to current repo. Use instead of grep when the question is fuzzy, cross-file, or about prior reasoning. Skip for pure navigation. ([LucasSantana-Dev/sharekit](https://github.com/LucasSantana-Dev/sharekit/tree/HEAD/sharekit-profile/.claude/skills/recall) · ★2)
- **recall**: Recalls what was said, decided, or tried in past coding-agent sessions on this machine, and reaches for that record before re-deriving something a past session already settled; use it when a request sounds like what did we decide about a topic, where did we leave off, have we hit this before, which session was that, or when did we first raise something. ([mayberuk/recall](https://github.com/mayberuk/recall/tree/HEAD/integrations/plugins/claude-code/skills/recall) · ★2 · 6 copies)
- **recall**: Spaced-repetition review of objectives the learner has already mastered (review mode). Use when the learner says "review", "revise", "quiz me on what I learned", or when status shows due_reviews. ([Nguyen1976/master-everything](https://github.com/Nguyen1976/master-everything/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/recall) · ★2)
- **recall**: Recover context from prior sessions in this repo. Use when the user points at earlier work, asks what happened last time, or resumes an unfinished task. ([notambourine/agent-plugins](https://github.com/notambourine/agent-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/nt-dev/skills/recall) · ★2)
- **recall**: Recall local memory without saving or changing it. ([petterssonjonas/tcui](https://github.com/petterssonjonas/tcui/tree/HEAD/skills/recall) · ★2)
- **recall**: Find what earlier sessions discussed or decided, from the saved session records and the repo .claude records (including superseded, rejected and archived ones). Use for "what did we decide before", "what problem did we have with X", "why was X decided or changed", "find the earlier discussion of Y". Not for facts that git log or the current files answer. 전에 뭐라고 정했지, 예전 논의 찾기, 그때 무슨 문제였지, 왜 이렇게 정했지. ([qwreey/qwreey-harness](https://github.com/qwreey/qwreey-harness/tree/HEAD/plugins/qwreey-sessions/skills/recall) · ★2)
- **recall**: Search Rembr long-term memory for relevant context from past sessions — user preferences, prior decisions, project facts. Use before re-deriving a decision or when the user asks "what did we decide about..." or "do you remember...". ([radicalgeek/rembr](https://github.com/radicalgeek/rembr/tree/HEAD/claude-code-rembr/skills/recall) · ★2)
- **recall**: Before unfamiliar project-area work; retrieve project decisions or pitfalls; save authorized durable fact; quirk just cost retry, backed-out change, or user correction → pitfalls. ([ryanportfolio/winuse-mcp](https://github.com/ryanportfolio/winuse-mcp/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/recall) · ★2 · 32 copies)
- **recall**: Search earlier BB threads and tasks by meaning with the Recall plugin (recall_search, recall_get, `bb recall`). Use before starting work to find past discussions, decisions and tickets on the same topic. ([sajov/bb-plugins](https://github.com/sajov/bb-plugins/tree/HEAD/bb-plugin-recall/skills/recall) · ★2)
- **recall**: Use to surface relevant prior sessions, decisions, patterns, and project facts from the local knowledge base. Returns ranked snippets with source paths — not full content. Treat results as navigation hints, not context. ([spencerneutron/Synapse](https://github.com/spencerneutron/Synapse/tree/HEAD/skills/recall) · ★2)
- **recall**: Pull relevant knowledge from your personal Markdown knowledge base on explicit request ("/recall", "check the memory", "what do we have on X"). Never auto-triggers. Searches knowledge files, follows continues_from/continues_in chains, detects multi-save files and reports by section. - **recall**: Search this machine's recorded Claude Code session history before answering "did we / have we / why is this like this / what did we decide" questions, when an error looks familiar, or before re-deriving something that looks like prior work. Uses `claude-transcripts search --json`; cites session ids; never loads a whole transcript. - **recall**: Reconstruct your recent working context from your own chat history, live state, and the shared record (user reports, prior fixes, incidents), then hand back a tight current-state b ([YeonwooSung/RavenClaw](https://github.com/YeonwooSung/RavenClaw/tree/HEAD/packages/core/src/skills/builtin/cursor-pack/catalog/recall) · ★2)
- **recall**: Deliberately recall what memory knows — "what do you remember about X", "what do you know here", the corpus by type — and the receipt for why a memory was or was not recalled. - **recall**: Search the shared eidetic memory store and get back ranked, provenanced records. Drives `eidetic recall` with four search modes — exact (verbatim substring), approximate (vector/semantic), keyword (BM25 lexical), and hybrid (a weighted blend of vector+keyword, the default) — each hit carrying its text, full metadata, a relevance `score`, and a freshness `signal`. Recall passively reinforces matched records (bumps last_recall + recall_count). Shadowed and archived records are excluded by default; use --include-shadowed / --include-archived to retrieve them. The store lives at ~/.eidetic/memory… ([agentculture/agentsgit](https://github.com/agentculture/agentsgit/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/recall) · ★1)

Recall passively reinforces matched records (bumps last_recall + recall_count). Shadowed and archived records are excluded by default; use --include-shadowed / --include-archived to retrieve them. - **recall**: Search the shared eidetic memory store and get back ranked, provenanced records. Drives `eidetic recall` with four search modes — exact (verbatim substring), approximate (vector/semantic), keyword (BM25 lexical), and hybrid (a weighted blend of vector+keyword, the default) — each hit carrying its text, full metadata, a relevance `score`, and a freshness `signal`. Recall passively reinforces matched records (bumps last_recall + recall_count). Shadowed and archived records are excluded by default; use --include-shadowed / --include-archived to retrieve them.
